   test('the CLI is not given a budget of its own: the relay is the only bound, and every stop is in its log (D-P13-10)', async () => {
     const provider = new RecordingProvider();
     provider.stdout = stream();
     await driverWith(provider).runTask(request());
     const argv = provider.calls.flatMap((call) => call.cmd);
     expect(argv).not.toContain('--max-budget-usd');
   });
 
   // Each tier names the exact id the CLI sends, and the line prices a call's relay for that id
   // alone; a tier whose model had no price would be refused before the call was made (D-R14-01).
   test('every tier resolves to an exact model id the meter prices, never an alias', () => {
     const driver = driverWith(new RecordingProvider());
     for (const tier of ['fast', 'standard', 'deep'] as const) {
       const { model } = driver.resolveModel(tier);
       expect(Object.hasOwn(MODEL_METER.prices, model), `${tier} resolves to ${model}`).toBe(true);
       expect(['haiku', 'sonnet', 'opus']).not.toContain(model);
     }
   });
 
   test('every setting through which the CLI picks a model of its own names the model of the call (D-R14-02)', async () => {
     const provider = new RecordingProvider();
     provider.stdout = stream();
     await driverWith(provider).runTask(request({ tier: 'deep' }));
     const call = taskCall(provider);
     const model = driverWith(provider).resolveModel('deep').model;
     expect(call?.cmd[call.cmd.indexOf('--model') + 1]).toBe(model);
     for (const name of ['ANTHROPIC_SMALL_FAST_MODEL', 'ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_HAIKU_MODEL', 'ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_SONNET_MODEL', 'ANTHROPIC_DEFAULT_OPUS_MODEL', 'CLAUDE_CODE_SUBAGENT_MODEL']) {
       expect(call?.env?.[name], name).toBe(model);
     }
   });
 
   test('the meter names at least one model, and every model it names carries all five prices, each a non-negative finite number', () => {
     expect(MODEL_METER.dialect).toBe('anthropic-messages');
     const fields = ['inputPerMTok', 'outputPerMTok', 'cacheReadPerMTok', 'cacheWritePerMTok', 'cacheWrite1hPerMTok'] as const;
     const models = Object.entries(MODEL_METER.prices);
     expect(models.length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
     for (const [model, prices] of models) {
       for (const field of fields) expect(Number.isFinite(prices[field]) && prices[field] >= 0, `${model}.${field}`).toBe(true);
     }
   });
 
   test('the prefix and the suffix reach different flags, which is what makes the cache reading possible', async () => {
     const provider = new RecordingProvider();

     await driverWith(provider).runTask(request());
     const cmd = provider.calls[0]?.cmd ?? [];
     expect(cmd[cmd.indexOf('--append-system-prompt') + 1]).toBe(`${BUILDER.instructions}\n\nthe invariant half`);
     expect(cmd[cmd.indexOf('--print') + 1]).toBe('this task only');
     expect(cmd).toContain('--exclude-dynamic-system-prompt-sections');
   });
 
   test('the granted tools reach --tools, which decides what exists rather than what needs approval (I4)', async () => {
     const provider = new RecordingProvider();

     await driverWith(provider).runTask(request({ tools: ['Read', 'Write'] }));
     const cmd = provider.calls[0]?.cmd ?? [];

     expect(cmd).not.toContain('--allowedTools');
   });
 
   test('an empty grant is an empty tool list, not an absent flag (I4)', async () => {
     const provider = new RecordingProvider();
     provider.stdout = stream({ tools: [] });
     await driverWith(provider).runTask(request({ tools: [] }));
     const cmd = provider.calls[0]?.cmd ?? [];
     expect(cmd.indexOf('--tools')).toBeGreaterThan(-1);
     expect(cmd[cmd.indexOf('--tools') + 1]).toBe('');
   });
 
   test('no settings source outside the argv configures the session (I3)', async () => {
     const provider = new RecordingProvider();
     provider.stdout = stream();

     const cmd = provider.calls[0]?.cmd ?? [];
     expect(cmd[cmd.indexOf('--setting-sources') + 1]).toBe('');
     expect(cmd).toContain('--strict-mcp-config');
   });

 export class CodexDriver implements Driver {
   readonly id = 'codex';
   readonly contractVersion = DRIVER_CONTRACT_VERSION;
 
   readonly #provider: SandboxProvider;
   readonly #workdir: string;
   readonly #models: Readonly<Record<ModelTier, string>>;
   readonly #inFlight = new Map<SandboxHandle, Promise<unknown>>();
   #running = 0;
   readonly #waiting: Array<() => void> = [];
   readonly #handlers = new Map<DriverEvent['kind'], Array<(e: DriverEvent) => void>>();
   readonly #inspections = new Map<TaskId, Inspection>();
 
   constructor(options: CodexDriverOptions) {
     // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unnecessary-condition -- the type requires a provider, but a missing one here means running the model on the host (I1). A caller from untyped code is refused, not trusted.
     if (options.provider === undefined || options.provider === null) {
       refuse('provider', 'CodexDriver needs the SandboxProvider that provisioned the handle; there is no host fallback (I1)');
     }
     const workdir = options.workdir ?? '/workspace';
     if (!SAFE_DIR.test(workdir)) {
       refuse('capability', `the working directory ${JSON.stringify(workdir)} is not a plain absolute path; it is named in the CLI's configuration, which is TOML`);
     }
     const models = options.models ?? TIER_MODELS;
     for (const [tier, model] of Object.entries(models)) {
       if (!Object.hasOwn(MODEL_METER.prices, model)) {
         refuse('capability', `tier '${tier}' maps to ${JSON.stringify(model)}, which MODEL_METER does not price, so the relay would refuse every call`);
       }
     }
     this.#provider = options.provider;
     this.#workdir = workdir;
     this.#models = models;
   }
 
   provenanceId(): string {
     return `${this.id}@${CLI_VERSION}`;
   }
 
   /**
    * Each value is asserted against the real CLI under `driver.<key>.codex`
    * (I8, D-R26-07). The `false` ones have no method behind them: a method
    * present beside a capability declared false is the direction the
    * conformance assertion refuses.
    */
   capabilities(): DriverCapabilities {
     return {
       subagents: false,
       hooks: false,
       mcp: false,
       parallelism: PARALLELISM,
       computerUse: false,
       steering: false,
       stablePrefixCaching: false,
     };
   }
 
   declaredTools(): readonly string[] {
     return DECLARED_TOOLS_VIEW;
   }
 
-  relayRequest(): Omit<RelaySpec, 'budget'> {
-    return { ...MODEL_RELAY, paths: [...MODEL_RELAY.paths] };
+  relayRequest(_tools: readonly string[]): Omit<RelaySpec, 'budget'> {
+    return { ...MODEL_RELAY, paths: [...MODEL_RELAY.paths], serverTools: [] };
   }
 
   /** I6: `family` is `FAMILY`, assigned. Nothing here reads the tier, the model, or `this.id` to produce it. */
   resolveModel(tier: ModelTier): ModelIdentity {
     const model = this.#models[tier];
     // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unnecessary-condition -- a tier from parsed configuration can be outside the union, and an undefined model would reach the argv as 'undefined'.
     if (model === undefined) refuse('capability', `no model is mapped to tier '${tier}'`);
     return { provider: 'openai', family: FAMILY, model, version: CLI_VERSION };
   }
 
   runTask(req: TaskRequest): Promise<TaskResult> {
     return this.#serialized(req.sandbox, () => this.#runTask(req));
   }
 
   /** Forgets a task's inspection. The container is the run's, so nothing here destroys it. */
   cancel(taskId: TaskId): Promise<void> {
     this.#inspections.delete(taskId);
     return Promise.resolve();
   }
 
   /** `hooks` is false: there is no artifact format this driver installs, so it refuses and writes nothing. */
   emitArtifacts(roles: CompiledRole[], targetDir: string): Promise<void> {
     return Promise.reject(
       new DriverRefusal(
         'capability',
         `emitArtifacts was asked for ${String(roles.length)} role(s) under ${targetDir}, but this driver declares hooks false and installs no artifacts; ` +
           'a role reaches the session on the argv alone (D-R26-08)',
       ),
     );
   }
 
   on(kind: DriverEvent['kind'], handler: (e: DriverEvent) => void): void {
     const existing = this.#handlers.get(kind);
     if (existing === undefined) this.#handlers.set(kind, [handler]);
     else existing.push(handler);
   }
 
   /** What the inspection read for a task, once one has run. */
   inspectionFor(taskId: TaskId): Inspection | undefined {
     return this.#inspections.get(taskId);
   }
 
   /**
    * Runs the inspection alone and returns what it read, refusing as a task
    * would. No model is called. The suite uses it to read the request a
    * session would send without paying for one.
    */
   inspect(req: TaskRequest): Promise<Inspection> {
     return this.#serialized(req.sandbox, async () => {
       const groups = this.#groupsOrRefuse(req);
       const identity = this.resolveModel(req.tier);
       return this.#inspect(req, identity, groups);
     });
   }
 
   async #serialized<T>(handle: SandboxHandle, body: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
     for (let held = this.#inFlight.get(handle); held !== undefined; held = this.#inFlight.get(handle)) {
       await held.catch(() => undefined);
     }
     const running = this.#bounded(body);
     this.#inFlight.set(handle, running);
     try {
       return await running;
     } finally {
       if (this.#inFlight.get(handle) === running) this.#inFlight.delete(handle);
     }
   }
 
   async #bounded<T>(body: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
     if (this.#running >= PARALLELISM) await new Promise<void>((admit) => this.#waiting.push(admit));
     else this.#running += 1;
     try {
       return await body();
     } finally {
       const next = this.#waiting.shift();
       if (next === undefined) this.#running -= 1;
       else next();
     }
   }
 
   async #runTask(req: TaskRequest): Promise<TaskResult> {
     const groups = this.#groupsOrRefuse(req);
     const identity = this.resolveModel(req.tier);
     await this.#inspect(req, identity, groups);
 
     const exec = await this.#provider.exec(req.sandbox, runArgv(this.#workdir, cliArgs(this.#workdir, identity.model, req, groups)), {
       env: { [KEY_VARIABLE]: KEY_PLACEHOLDER },
     });
     const parsed = parseEvents(exec.stdout);
     refuseUnusable(req, exec.exitCode, exec.stderr, parsed, identity);
     const events = eventsFrom(parsed);
     for (const event of events) this.#emit(event);
     return {
       taskId: req.taskId,
       claim: claimFrom(parsed),
       events,
       usage: usageFrom(parsed, identity.model, exec.durationMs),
       // The CLI reports no model of its own (D-R26-01); a reroute it did report was refused above.
       model: identity,
       contractVersion: this.contractVersion,
     };
   }
 
   /** I4: the grant is refused unless the CLI can offer exactly it. */
   #groupsOrRefuse(req: TaskRequest): readonly ToolGroup[] {
     const mcp = req.tools.filter((tool) => tool.startsWith('mcp__'));
     if (mcp.length > 0) {
       refuse('grant', `the task grants ${mcp.map((t) => `'${t}'`).join(', ')}, but this driver declares mcp false and configures no server (D-R26-07)`);
     }
     const result = groupsFor(req.tools);
     if ('refusal' in result) {
       refuse('grant', `${result.refusal}. The task was not started: a grant this driver cannot honour exactly means something other than what it says (I4).`);
     }
     return result.groups;
   }
 
   /** D-R26-02: the request the session would send, read from an endpoint on the sandbox's loopback, and held to the grant. */
   async #inspect(req: TaskRequest, identity: ModelIdentity, groups: readonly ToolGroup[]): Promise<Inspection> {
     const nonce = crypto.randomUUID();
     const exec = await this.#provider.exec(req.sandbox, inspectArgv(this.#workdir, nonce, cliArgs(this.#workdir, identity.model, req, groups)), {
       env: { [KEY_VARIABLE]: KEY_PLACEHOLDER, [CAPTURE_SOURCE_VARIABLE]: CAPTURE_SOURCE, [CAPTURE_NONCE_VARIABLE]: nonce, NO_PROXY: '127.0.0.1', no_proxy: '127.0.0.1' },
     });
     const captured = capturedFrom(exec.stdout, nonce);
     if ('missing' in captured) {
       refuse(
         'invocation',
         `the session for task ${req.taskId} could not be inspected: ${captured.missing} (exit code ${String(exec.exitCode)})` +
           (exec.stderr.trim() === '' ? '' : `; stderr: ${exec.stderr.trim().slice(0, 400)}`) +
           '. The task was not started: running it would offer the model tools nobody has enumerated (I4, I5).',
       );
     }
     const inspection = inspectionFrom(req, captured.requests);
     const granted = [...new Set(req.tools)].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
     const offered = [...new Set(inspection.tools)].sort((a, b) => a.localeCompare(b));
     const ungranted = offered.filter((tool) => !granted.includes(tool));
     const missing = granted.filter((tool) => !offered.includes(tool));
     if (ungranted.length > 0 || missing.length > 0 || offered.length !== inspection.tools.length) {
       refuse(
         'grant',
         `the session for task ${req.taskId} would offer [${inspection.tools.join(', ')}] where the request granted [${granted.join(', ')}]` +
           (ungranted.length > 0 ? `; ${ungranted.join(', ')} not granted (I4)` : '') +
           (missing.length > 0 ? `; ${missing.join(', ')} granted and absent, so the grant did not take effect (I5)` : ''),
       );
     }
     if (inspection.model !== identity.model) {
       refuse('invocation', `the session for task ${req.taskId} would ask for ${JSON.stringify(inspection.model)} where the runtime resolved ${identity.model}`);
     }
     if (inspection.authorization !== `Bearer ${KEY_PLACEHOLDER}`) {
       refuse(
         'credential',
         `the session for task ${req.taskId} would authenticate with something other than the placeholder in ${KEY_VARIABLE}; ` +
           'a key the driver did not supply is one it cannot account for, and one the CLI found is a credential inside the sandbox',
       );
     }
     this.#inspections.set(req.taskId, inspection);
     return inspection;
   }
 
   #emit(event: DriverEvent): void {
     for (const handler of this.#handlers.get(event.kind) ?? []) handler(event);
   }
 }
 
 /** What `declaredTools()` hands out: a frozen copy, so a caller cannot widen the inventory. */

 export class OpenAICompatibleDriver implements Driver {
   readonly id = 'openai-compatible';
   readonly contractVersion = DRIVER_CONTRACT_VERSION;
 
   readonly #provider: SandboxProvider;
   readonly #config: CheckedConfig;
   readonly #workdir: string;
   readonly #credential: string;
   /** One task per sandbox at a time, whatever `parallelism` says (A-R4-04). */
   readonly #inFlight = new Map<SandboxHandle, Promise<unknown>>();
   /** Tasks running now across sandboxes, and those waiting for a slot, in arrival order. */
   #running = 0;
   readonly #waiting: Array<() => void> = [];
   readonly #handlers = new Map<DriverEvent['kind'], Array<(e: DriverEvent) => void>>();
   readonly #cancelled = new Set<TaskId>();
   /** Every request body each task sent, kept so a caller — and a conformance assertion — can read what the model was offered. */
   readonly #requests = new Map<TaskId, Array<Record<string, unknown>>>();
 
   constructor(options: OpenAICompatibleDriverOptions) {
     // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unnecessary-condition -- the type requires a provider, but a missing one means running tools on the host (I1). A caller arriving from untyped code is refused, not trusted.
     if (options.provider === undefined || options.provider === null) {
       refuse('provider', 'OpenAICompatibleDriver needs the SandboxProvider that provisioned the handle; there is no host fallback (I1)');
     }
     if (typeof options.provider.relayCall !== 'function') {
       refuse(
         'provider',
         `the provider '${options.provider.id}' cannot call the model relay from outside the sandbox; a model call made from inside it is answered by whatever the task left there (A-R6-05)`,
       );
     }
     this.#provider = options.provider;
     this.#config = checkConfig(options.endpoint);
     this.#workdir = options.workdir ?? '/workspace';
     this.#credential = options.credential ?? ENDPOINT_CREDENTIAL;
   }
 
   provenanceId(): string {
     return `${this.id}@${DRIVER_VERSION} ${this.#config.provider}/${this.#config.version}`;
   }
 
   /**
    * Every feature is false, and each is proven absent (D-R6-06): an endpoint
    * the driver has not measured backs no `true`. `parallelism` is the
    * operator's number, and `#bounded` is what makes it true.
    */
   capabilities(): DriverCapabilities {
     return {
       subagents: false,
       hooks: false,
       mcp: false,
       parallelism: this.#config.parallelism,
       computerUse: false,
       steering: false,
       stablePrefixCaching: false,
     };
   }
 
   declaredTools(): readonly string[] {
     return DECLARED_TOOLS;
   }
 
   /** The relay every sandbox this driver runs in is provisioned with: the endpoint's origin, its one path, and the `openai-chat` meter (A-R6-01). */
-  relayRequest(): Omit<RelaySpec, 'budget'> {
+  relayRequest(_tools: readonly string[]): Omit<RelaySpec, 'budget'> {
     return {
       upstream: this.#config.upstream,
       paths: [this.#config.path],
       header: this.#config.header,
       scheme: this.#config.scheme,
       // A-R21-02: an endpoint declared keyless gets a declared null, never a name the provider may not hold.
       credential: this.#config.scheme === 'none' ? null : this.#credential,
       urlVariable: RELAY_URL_VARIABLE,
       meter: { dialect: 'openai-chat', prices: { ...this.#config.prices } },
+      // A-R56-01: none. The chat dialect refuses every hosted tool (D-P13-12), and no declared tool is one.
+      serverTools: [],
     };
   }
 
   /** I6: the family is the operator's declaration, branded once in the constructor. Nothing here derives it. */
   resolveModel(tier: ModelTier): ModelIdentity {
     const model = this.#config.models[tier];
     // eslint-disable-next-line @typescript-eslint/no-unnecessary-condition -- ModelTier is a union, but a tier arriving from parsed configuration can be outside it.
     if (model === undefined) refuse('capability', `no model is mapped to tier '${tier}'`);
     return { provider: this.#config.provider, family: this.#config.family, model, version: this.#config.version };
   }
 
   runTask(req: TaskRequest): Promise<TaskResult> {
     return this.#serialized(req.sandbox, () => this.#runTask(req));
   }
 
   /**
    * `spawnSubagent` and `steer` are deliberately absent, because
    * `capabilities()` declares both false. A method that threw would be a
    * capability declared false and present.
    */
 
   /** Stops a task at its next turn. The sandbox is the run's, so nothing here destroys it. */
   cancel(taskId: TaskId): Promise<void> {
     this.#cancelled.add(taskId);
     return Promise.resolve();
   }
 
   /**
    * There is no artifact format: the role is rendered on each request and
    * nowhere else (D-R6-06), so there is nothing to write and no hook to point
    * at. Refused rather than a no-op, which would read as roles written.
    */
   emitArtifacts(roles: CompiledRole[], targetDir: string): Promise<void> {
     return Promise.reject(
       new DriverRefusal(
         'capability',
         `OpenAICompatibleDriver has no artifact format; ${String(roles.length)} role(s) for ${targetDir} were not written. ` +
           'A role reaches this driver on each request, never as a file (D-R6-06).',
       ),
     );
   }
 
   on(kind: DriverEvent['kind'], handler: (e: DriverEvent) => void): void {
     const existing = this.#handlers.get(kind);
     if (existing === undefined) this.#handlers.set(kind, [handler]);
     else existing.push(handler);
   }
 
   /** Every request body a task sent to the endpoint, in order. */
   requestsFor(taskId: TaskId): ReadonlyArray<Readonly<Record<string, unknown>>> {
     return this.#requests.get(taskId) ?? [];
   }
 
   async #serialized<T>(handle: SandboxHandle, body: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
     // Checked again after every wait: another follower may have claimed the handle first.
     for (let held = this.#inFlight.get(handle); held !== undefined; held = this.#inFlight.get(handle)) {
       await held.catch(() => undefined);
     }
     const running = this.#bounded(body);
     this.#inFlight.set(handle, running);
     try {
       return await running;
     } finally {
       if (this.#inFlight.get(handle) === running) this.#inFlight.delete(handle);
     }
   }
 
   async #bounded<T>(body: () => Promise<T>): Promise<T> {
     if (this.#running >= this.#config.parallelism) await new Promise<void>((admit) => this.#waiting.push(admit));
     else this.#running += 1;
     try {
       return await body();
     } finally {
       // A slot passes straight to the next caller waiting, so a newcomer never jumps the queue.
       const next = this.#waiting.shift();
       if (next === undefined) this.#running -= 1;
       else next();
     }
   }
 
   #emit(event: DriverEvent): void {
     for (const handler of this.#handlers.get(event.kind) ?? []) handler(event);
   }
 
   /**
    * I4: a granted tool this driver does not declare is refused before the
    * task starts, so a caller that bypassed the policy engine gets no wider
    * session than policy would have allowed.
    */
   #refuseUngrantedTools(req: TaskRequest): void {
     const declared = new Set(DECLARED_TOOLS);
     const undeclared = req.tools.filter((tool) => !declared.has(tool));
     if (undeclared.length > 0) {
       refuse(
         'grant',
         `the task grants ${undeclared.map((t) => `'${t}'`).join(', ')}, which this driver does not declare. ` +
           'The task was not started: a grant this driver cannot honour is a policy that means something other than what it says (I4).',
       );
     }
   }
 
   async #runTask(req: TaskRequest): Promise<TaskResult> {
     this.#refuseUngrantedTools(req);
     this.#cancelled.delete(req.taskId);
     const identity = this.resolveModel(req.tier);
     const granted = new Set(req.tools);
     const tools = definitionsFor(req.tools);
     const started = Date.now();
     const deadline = started + req.timeoutMs;
     const exec = (argv: string[], options?: ExecOptions): Promise<ExecResult> => this.#provider.exec(req.sandbox, argv, options);
 
     // The role and the run's stable material form the system message, and this task's own text the user
     // message, as the Claude Code driver renders them (D-A-RC-03): instructions first, never a file.
     const messages: ChatMessage[] = [
       { role: 'system', content: `${req.role.instructions}\n\n${req.stablePrefix}` },
       { role: 'user', content: req.variableSuffix },
     ];
     const events: DriverEvent[] = [];
     const written = new Set<string>();
     const usage = { inputTokens: 0, outputTokens: 0, cacheReadTokens: 0, cacheWriteTokens: 0, costUsd: 0 };
     const sent: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
     this.#requests.set(req.taskId, sent);
     const price = this.#config.prices[identity.model];
     // checkConfig refuses a tier model with no price; the table is a record, so the index is checked again here.
     if (price === undefined) refuse('configuration', `the model ${identity.model} has no price`);
 
     for (let turn = 1; ; turn += 1) {
       if (this.#cancelled.has(req.taskId)) refuse('invocation', `task ${req.taskId} was cancelled`);
       if (Date.now() >= deadline) refuse('invocation', `task ${req.taskId} ran past its ${String(req.timeoutMs)} ms bound`);
       if (turn > this.#config.maxTurns) {
         refuse('invocation', `task ${req.taskId} did not finish in ${String(this.#config.maxTurns)} model calls, the configured maxTurns`);
       }
 
       const body: Record<string, unknown> = {
         model: identity.model,
         messages,
         [this.#config.ceilingField]: this.#config.maxOutputTokens,
         stream: false,
         ...(tools.length === 0 ? {} : { tools }),
       };
       sent.push(JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(body)) as Record<string, unknown>);
       const answer = await this.#call(req.sandbox, body);
       const reply = readTurn(answer);
       if (reply.model !== identity.model) {
         refuse(
           'identity',
           `the endpoint answered as ${JSON.stringify(reply.model)} when ${JSON.stringify(identity.model)} was requested. ` +
             `The family '${identity.family}' was declared for the model requested, so the answer is not one the run can attribute (I6, D-R6-03).`,
         );
       }
       const input = reply.promptTokens - reply.cachedTokens;
       usage.inputTokens += input;
       usage.cacheReadTokens += reply.cachedTokens;
       usage.outputTokens += reply.completionTokens;
       usage.costUsd += (input * price.inputPerMTok + reply.cachedTokens * price.cacheReadPerMTok + reply.completionTokens * price.outputPerMTok) / 1e6;
 
       if (reply.toolCalls.length === 0) {
         const result: TaskResult = {
           taskId: req.taskId,
           claim: { narrative: reply.content ?? '', filesChanged: [...written].map((path) => this.#claimed(path)) },
           events,
           usage: { ...usage, wallClockMs: Date.now() - started } satisfies Usage,
           model: identity,
           contractVersion: this.contractVersion,
         };
         return result;
       }
 
       messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: reply.content, tool_calls: reply.toolCalls });
       for (const call of reply.toolCalls) {
         const name = call.function.name;
         let output: string;
         if (!granted.has(name)) {
           // Never run, and recorded: the model asked for a tool policy did not give it (I4).
           output = `error: the tool '${name}' is not granted to this task`;
           const refused: DriverEvent = { at: new Date().toISOString(), kind: 'tool-call', detail: { tool: name, refused: true } };
           events.push(refused);
           this.#emit(refused);
         } else {
           const args = argumentsOf(call);
           const outcome: ToolOutcome =
             typeof args === 'string'
               ? { output: args, event: { at: new Date().toISOString(), kind: 'tool-call' as const, detail: { tool: name, error: args } } }
               : await runTool(name, args, exec, this.#workdir);
           output = outcome.output;
           events.push(outcome.event);
           this.#emit(outcome.event);
           if (outcome.written !== undefined) written.add(outcome.written);
         }
         messages.push({ role: 'tool', tool_call_id: call.id, content: output });
       }
     }
   }
 
   /** One model call through the relay, made from the relay's own container (A-R6-05). Returns the body of a success; refuses on anything else. */
   async #call(sandbox: SandboxHandle, body: Record<string, unknown>): Promise<string> {
     const provider = this.#provider;
     // The constructor refused a provider without relayCall; the field is optional in the type, so it is checked again here.
     if (provider.relayCall === undefined) refuse('provider', `the provider '${provider.id}' cannot call the model relay (A-R6-05)`);
     let answer: { readonly status: number; readonly body: string };
     try {
       answer = await provider.relayCall(sandbox, { path: this.#config.path, body: JSON.stringify(body) });
     } catch (error) {
       // The sandbox's own refusals (its wall clock, its output cap) are reported as the sandbox's.
       if (error instanceof DriverRefusal || error instanceof SandboxRefusal) throw error;
       refuse('invocation', `the model call through the relay failed: ${error instanceof Error ? error.message : String(error)}`);
     }
     if (answer.status === 402) refuse('budget', `the relay refused the call: ${quoted(answer.body)}`);
     if (answer.status < 200 || answer.status >= 300) {
       refuse('endpoint', `the endpoint answered ${String(answer.status)}: ${quoted(answer.body)}`);
     }
     return answer.body;
   }
 
   /** A written path as the claim names it: relative to the working directory when inside it. */
   #claimed(path: string): string {
     const relative = posix.relative(this.#workdir, path);
     return relative.startsWith('..') || posix.isAbsolute(relative) ? path : relative;
   }
 }

   certificate ??= await makeCertificate();

         if (relay === undefined) throw new Error('the sandbox was provisioned without its relay');

         return { handle, workspace };
       },
       request: (handle, overrides = {}) => ({
         taskId: 'task' as TaskId,

         budget: BUDGET,

     await run('docker', ['rm', '--force', endpointName]).catch(() => undefined);
     await run('docker', ['network', 'rm', network]).catch(() => undefined);

     // host: on a GitHub runner, which is uid 1001, the host cannot delete what
     // the task wrote. Root in a throwaway container can. On a developer's
     // machine the two usually coincide and this changes nothing.
     await run('docker', ['run', '--rm', '--user', '0:0', '--entrypoint', 'sh', '--volume', `${base}:/clean`, EGRESS_PROXY_IMAGE, '-c', 'rm -rf /clean/* /clean/.[!.]*']).catch(() => undefined);
     await rm(base, { recursive: true, force: true });
   }
 }

 export const METER_PREFIX = 'model-relay-meter ';
 
 /**
  * The relay itself. Passed to `node --eval` as one argv element, so there is
  * no shell, no mount, and no image build between this source and what runs.
  *
  * It fails closed at start: no credential, no header, an upstream that is not
  * an `https` origin, an empty grant, no budget, a meter for a dialect it does
  * not read, a price table it cannot charge from, or no port each exit non-zero
  * rather than start a server, so a misconfigured relay is a sandbox that never
  * comes up rather than one whose model calls fail later for a reason nothing
  * recorded.
  *
  * Metering (P13), for the `anthropic-messages` dialect:
  *
  * - `POST /v1/messages` is metered. The request body is buffered to read its
  *   `model` and `max_tokens`, and forwarded byte for byte; a model the price
  *   table does not name is refused before anything is sent (D-P13-04). The
  *   response is streamed back unchanged while a copy is read for its usage.
+ * - A request offering a typed tool its grant's `serverTools` does not name,
+ *   or naming `mcp_servers` or a `container`, is refused before anything is
+ *   sent; so is one offering a granted tool with no positive `max_uses`. Each
+ *   use `usage.server_tool_use` reports is charged at its grant's price, and a
+ *   success reporting none is charged `max_uses` (A-R56-01).
  * - `POST /v1/messages/count_tokens`, `GET`, and `HEAD` cost nothing upstream
  *   and are forwarded unmetered. Every other request is refused: a write whose
  *   cost the meter cannot count is spend the budget cannot see (D-P13-12).
  * - One metered call is in flight at a time, so a call begun under budget ends
  *   over it by at most one call, as D-P13-03 states (D-P13-13).
  * - After each response the totals are updated; once the tokens (all four
  *   classes, D-P13-05) or the dollars reach their bound, every later request is
  *   refused with 402 and `x-should-retry: false`, naming the bound.
  * - A successful response with its input usage and no final output usage is
  *   charged `max_tokens` as output; one with no readable usage at all is
  *   charged `max_tokens` as output, marks the budget `unreadable`, and stops
  *   the relay forwarding. A response in a content encoding the relay did not
  *   ask for is unreadable. A response that is not a success is charged nothing.
  * - Under the `openai-chat` dialect (A-R6-01) the same rules hold with that
  *   API's names: a `POST` to a granted path ending `/chat/completions` is
  *   metered and a `GET` is free; the ceiling is the larger of
  *   `max_completion_tokens` and `max_tokens`, times `n`; the usage is
  *   `prompt_tokens` (less its cached part, counted as cache reads) and
  *   `completion_tokens`, from the body or from the stream chunk that carries it.
  *   A request asking for a service tier other than the default, a web search,
  *   audio, or a tool the API runs itself is refused unsent: a tier is billed
  *   at another rate, a search or a hosted tool per call, and audio at audio
  *   rates inside the same prompt_tokens and completion_tokens the table
  *   charges at one rate (#88, gemini-3). A request naming no tier is served at
  *   the upstream's own default, so an answer reporting a tier other than the
  *   default is counted as read and then stops the relay forwarding, as an
  *   unreadable one does (codex-1). An answer naming no tier, as a local

 /**
  * Checks a relay request before any container starts, and refuses every one
  * the provider could not apply exactly as written (I5).
  *
  * `held` is the names of the credentials the provider was given. A request
  * naming any other is refused here: there is no value to put in the relay,
  * and a relay started without one would be a sandbox whose every model call
  * failed with an authentication error that says nothing about the provider.
  * `hostPorts` is the Docker host ports it was given, and a local upstream on
  * any other is refused the same way (A-R21-03).
  */
 export function checkRelay(relay: RelaySpec, held: ReadonlySet<string>, hostPorts: ReadonlySet<number>): RelayPlan {
   // Read as unknown: a spec arriving from untyped code may carry anything here.
   const declared: unknown = relay.upstream;
   const local = typeof declared === 'string' ? null : checkLocalUpstream(declared, hostPorts);
   let upstream: string;
   let host: string;
   if (local === null) {
     upstream = declared as string;
     let origin: URL | undefined;
     try {
       origin = new URL(upstream);
     } catch {
       origin = undefined;
     }
     const named = origin === undefined ? undefined : normalizeHost(origin.hostname);
     if (
       origin?.protocol !== 'https:' ||
       origin.origin !== upstream ||
       named === undefined ||
       named.includes(':') ||
       /^[0-9.]+$/u.test(named)
     ) {
       refuse(
         'relay',
         `relay.upstream must be an https origin naming a host, with no path, query, credentials, or trailing slash — ` +
           `'https://api.example.com' — not ${JSON.stringify(upstream)}. The relay verifies the upstream's certificate against that name, ` +
           'and anything else is a destination it cannot vouch for. A model on the Docker host is declared as ' +
           "{ local: 'docker-host', port }, never by an http URL (A-R21-01).",
       );
     }
     host = named;
   } else {
     upstream = `http://${LOCAL_UPSTREAM_ALIAS}:${String(local.port)}`;
     host = LOCAL_UPSTREAM_ALIAS;
   }
 
   if (!Array.isArray(relay.paths) || relay.paths.length === 0) {
     refuse('relay', 'relay.paths is empty. A relay that forwarded every path would hand the task the whole credential, so an empty grant is refused rather than read as everything.');
   }
   const bad = relay.paths.filter((p) => typeof p !== 'string' || !PATH_PREFIX.test(p));
   if (bad.length > 0) {
     refuse(
       'relay',
       `relay.paths must each be a path prefix of plain segments — no dot segment, no percent-encoding, no query, no trailing slash. ` +
         `Refused: ${bad.map((p) => JSON.stringify(p)).join(', ')}.`,
     );
   }
 
   if (!HEADER_NAME.test(relay.header) || RESERVED_HEADERS.has(relay.header.toLowerCase())) {
     refuse('relay', `relay.header ${JSON.stringify(relay.header)} is not a header a credential can be written to`);
   }
   // Read as unknown: a spec arriving from untyped code may carry no scheme, and none is refused rather than read as raw (A-R6-02).
   const scheme: unknown = (relay as { scheme?: unknown }).scheme;
   if (scheme !== 'raw' && scheme !== 'bearer' && scheme !== 'none') {
     refuse('relay', `relay.scheme must be 'raw', 'bearer', or 'none', not ${shown(scheme)}; a relay never guesses how its endpoint reads a credential`);
   }
   // A-R21-02: none is declared twice over, by a null credential and the none scheme, and only for a local upstream.

   if (credential === null) {
     if (scheme !== 'none') {

     if (local === null) {
       refuse('relay', 'relay.credential is null with an https upstream; a relay forwards without a credential only to a local endpoint on the Docker host, never to a remote origin');
     }
   } else if (typeof credential !== 'string' || credential === '') {
     refuse('relay', `relay.credential must name a credential the provider holds, or be null to declare none; ${shown(credential)} is neither, and an absent credential is never read as none`);
   } else if (scheme === 'none') {
     refuse('relay', `relay.credential names ${JSON.stringify(credential)} under the 'none' scheme; a relay is never handed a value it was told not to write`);
   }
   if (!ENV_NAME.test(relay.urlVariable) || RESERVED_VARIABLES.has(relay.urlVariable)) {
     refuse('relay', `relay.urlVariable ${JSON.stringify(relay.urlVariable)} is not a plain environment-variable name the provider leaves unset`);
   }
   const budget = checkBudget(relay.budget);
   const meter = checkMeter(relay.meter);
+  const serverTools = checkServerTools((relay as { serverTools?: unknown }).serverTools, meter.dialect);
   if (credential !== null && !held.has(credential)) {

         'A credential reaches a relay only from LocalDockerOptions.credentials; a spec names one and never carries a value.',
     );
   }

     upstreamHost: host,
     local: local === null ? null : local.local,

+    serverTools,

+/** I4, I5: a grant of server tools the relay can count and charge, under the one dialect that forwards any; refused whole otherwise. */
+function checkServerTools(grant: unknown, dialect: RelayDialect): readonly ServerToolGrant[] {
+  if (!Array.isArray(grant)) {
+    refuse('relay', `relay.serverTools must be a list of { type, perUseUsd }, empty to grant none, not ${shown(grant)}; an absent grant is never read as none or as all`);
+  }
+  const seen = new Set<string>();
+  const out: ServerToolGrant[] = [];
+  for (const entry of grant as unknown[]) {
+    if (!isRecord(entry) || Object.keys(entry).some((k) => k !== 'type' && k !== 'perUseUsd')) {
+      refuse('relay', `relay.serverTools holds ${shown(entry)}; each entry is { type, perUseUsd } and names nothing else`);
+    }
+    const { type, perUseUsd } = entry;
+    if (typeof type !== 'string' || !Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(COUNTED_SERVER_TOOLS, type)) {
+      refuse('relay', `relay.serverTools names ${shown(type)}, a server tool this relay cannot count; it counts ${Object.keys(COUNTED_SERVER_TOOLS).join(', ')}, and a use it could not count is spend the budget cannot see (D-P13-12)`);
+    }
+    if (typeof perUseUsd !== 'number' || !Number.isFinite(perUseUsd) || perUseUsd < 0) {
+      refuse('relay', `relay.serverTools prices ${type} at ${shown(perUseUsd)}; a use's price is a non-negative finite number of US dollars`);
+    }
+    if (seen.has(type)) refuse('relay', `relay.serverTools names ${type} twice`);
+    seen.add(type);
+    out.push({ type, perUseUsd });
+  }
+  if (out.length > 0 && dialect !== 'anthropic-messages') {
+    refuse('relay', `relay.serverTools grants ${[...seen].join(', ')} under the ${dialect} dialect, which refuses every tool the API runs itself (D-P13-12); a grant the relay would ignore is not started`);
+  }
+  return out;
+}

 /**
  * Starts the relay for one sandbox.
  *
  * With `shared`, the relay joins networks the proxy created. Without it, the
  * relay creates its own — an internal network with no default route, which the
  * sandbox will join, and an outbound bridge the sandbox never joins — exactly
  * as the proxy does, and removes them when it goes.
  *
  * The credential reaches the container as `--env RELAY_CREDENTIAL` with the
  * value set on the `docker` process, so it is in no argument vector on the
  * host. The daemon does keep the value in the relay container's own
  * configuration, readable with `docker inspect` on the host: the host is the
  * runtime's and inside the trust boundary, and the sandbox has no Docker
  * socket to ask. `trust` is extra root certificates, PEM, for an upstream a private
  * authority signed; it is not secret and travels the same way only because a
  * PEM block is unreadable in an argv.
  *
  * Anything that fails takes the whole attempt down with it.
  */
 export async function startRelay(
   id: string,
   plan: RelayPlan,
   credential: string | null,
   trust: string | undefined,
   shared: SharedNetworks | undefined,
   options: RelayOptions,
 ): Promise<AppliedRelay> {
   const names = relayNames(id);
   const created: string[] = [];
   let containerId = '';
 
   const run = async (args: string[], env?: Record<string, string>): Promise<string> => {
     const result = await dockerCli(options.executable, args, { timeoutMs: options.timeoutMs, ...(env === undefined ? {} : { env }) });
     if (result.exitCode !== 0) {
       throw new Error(`docker ${args[0] ?? ''} exited ${String(result.exitCode)}: ${result.stderr.trim() || result.stdout.trim()}`);
     }
     return result.stdout.trim();
   };
 
   const internal = shared?.internal ?? names.internal;
   const outbound = shared?.outbound ?? names.outbound;
   try {
     if (shared === undefined) {
       await createSandboxNetwork(run, names.internal, created, options.labels);
       await run(['network', 'create', '--driver', 'bridge', ...options.labels, names.outbound]);
       created.push(names.outbound);
     }
 
     // Under none there is no value, and no variable for one (A-R21-02).
     const secrets: Record<string, string> = credential === null ? {} : { RELAY_CREDENTIAL: credential };
     if (trust !== undefined) secrets.RELAY_TRUST = trust;
     const runArgs = [
       'run', '--detach', '--init',
       '--name', names.container,
       ...options.labels,
       '--network', internal,
       '--network-alias', RELAY_ALIAS,
       // A-R21-01: the route to the Docker host, given to this container and to no sandbox.
       ...(plan.local === null ? [] : ['--add-host', `${LOCAL_UPSTREAM_ALIAS}:host-gateway`, '--env', `RELAY_LOCAL=${plan.local}`]),
       '--env', `RELAY_UPSTREAM=${plan.upstream}`,
       '--env', `RELAY_PATHS=${JSON.stringify(plan.paths)}`,
       '--env', `RELAY_HEADER=${plan.header}`,
       '--env', `RELAY_SCHEME=${plan.scheme}`,
       '--env', `RELAY_PORT=${String(RELAY_PORT)}`,
       '--env', `RELAY_BUDGET=${JSON.stringify(plan.budget)}`,
       '--env', `RELAY_METER=${JSON.stringify(plan.meter)}`,
+      '--env', `RELAY_TOOLS=${JSON.stringify(plan.serverTools)}`,
       // Names only: the values are on the `docker` process, never in this argv.
       ...Object.keys(secrets).flatMap((name) => ['--env', name]),
       '--read-only',
       '--cap-drop', 'ALL',
       options.image,
       'node', '--eval', RELAY_SOURCE,
     ];
     containerId = await run(runArgs, secrets);
     if (containerId === '') throw new Error('docker run reported no container id for the model relay');
 
     // Attached after the container exists, so the relay is never briefly on a network the sandbox can reach.
     await run(['network', 'connect', outbound, containerId]);
 
     await requireListening(containerId, options);
 
     return {
       containerId,
       name: names.container,
       upstream: plan.upstream,
       local: plan.local,
       paths: Object.freeze([...plan.paths]),
       header: plan.header,
       scheme: plan.scheme,
       credential: plan.credential,
       urlVariable: plan.urlVariable,
       budget: plan.budget,
       meter: plan.meter,
+      serverTools: plan.serverTools,
       endpoint: `${RELAY_ALIAS}:${String(RELAY_PORT)}`,
       internalNetwork: internal,
       outboundNetwork: outbound,
       ownsNetworks: shared === undefined,
       runArgs: Object.freeze([...runArgs]),
     };
   } catch (error) {
     throw withLeftovers(error, await teardown(options, containerId === '' ? names.container : containerId, created));
   }
 }

===== packages/sandbox/src/types.ts =====
diff --git a/packages/sandbox/src/types.ts b/packages/sandbox/src/types.ts
index f0c98df..1e65bfe 100644
--- a/packages/sandbox/src/types.ts
+++ b/packages/sandbox/src/types.ts
@@ -28,63 +28,79 @@ export interface EgressPolicy { mode: 'deny-all' | 'allowlist'; allow: string[];
 /**
  * A model relay: a provider-owned endpoint that holds a credential the sandbox
  * never does, and forwards the sandbox's requests to one upstream with it
  * (A-P12-01).
  *
  * The spec names the credential and never carries it, because a `SandboxSpec`
  * is a record the runtime may keep and a secret in it is a secret in the
  * record. An implementation resolves `credential` against the credentials it
  * was constructed with.
  *
  * Implementations MUST keep the credential out of the sandbox — out of its
  * environment, its mounts, and every process it runs — and MUST refuse a relay
  * whose credential they were not given, whose `upstream` is not an `https`
  * origin, or whose `paths` is empty, rather than provision a sandbox whose
  * model calls fail later for a reason nothing recorded (I5). An implementation
  * that cannot keep a credential out of a process it runs MUST refuse every
  * spec that carries a relay.
  *
  * A relay is independent of `egress` (D-P12-02): under `deny-all` the sandbox
  * reaches the relay and nothing else. Under `allowlist` the relay is still the
  * only route to its upstream, so implementations MUST refuse an `egress.allow`
  * naming the upstream's host (D-P12-11).
  */
 export interface RelaySpec {
   /**
    * The only origin the relay reaches: an `https` origin with no path,
    * `'https://api.example.com'`, or a local endpoint on the Docker host
    * (A-R21-01). The second is the only way a relay speaks `http`; a URL is
    * never read as local, whatever host it names.
    */
   upstream: string | LocalUpstream;
   /** The path prefixes forwarded, each a segment boundary. Every other path is refused. Never empty (D-P12-04). */
   paths: string[];
   /** The one request header the credential is written into. Every authentication header the client sent is discarded. */
   header: string;
   /**
    * How the credential is written into `header` (A-R6-02): `'raw'` as it is,
    * `'bearer'` as `Bearer <credential>`, `'none'` not at all, with the
    * client's value in `header` deleted (A-R21-02). Required, so no relay writes
    * a scheme its endpoint did not ask for; any other value is refused.
    */
   scheme: RelayScheme;
   /**
    * The name of a credential the provider holds, never a value; or `null`,
    * declaring the endpoint takes none (A-R21-02). `null` comes with the
    * `'none'` scheme and a local upstream, or the spec is refused. An absent
    * credential is refused, never read as none.
    */
   credential: string | null;
   /** The environment variable the provider sets in the sandbox to the relay's address, so no caller hard-codes it. */
   urlVariable: string;
   /**
    * What one driver call may spend through this relay (A-P13-01). The relay
    * counts what each response used and refuses every request once either
    * bound is reached, so a call begun under budget may end over it by at most
    * one call (D-P13-03). Each bound MUST be a positive finite number.
    */
   budget: RelayBudget;
   /** How the relay reads what a call used and prices it. A dialect the implementation does not know is refused (D-P13-06). */
   meter: RelayMeter;
+  /**
+   * The tools the API runs itself that a request through this relay may offer
+   * (A-R56-01), derived by the driver from the task's grant. Empty grants none.
+   * Under `'anthropic-messages'` a request whose `tools` name any typed tool
+   * not listed here, or that carries `mcp_servers` or `container`, is refused
+   * unsent; each use of a listed tool is charged its price. Implementations
+   * MUST refuse a type they cannot count, and any entry under another dialect,
+   * whose hosted tools are refused outright (D-P13-12).
+   */
+  serverTools: readonly ServerToolGrant[];
+}
+
+/** One server tool a relay forwards, by the exact `type` a request names (`'web_search_20250305'`), and what one use costs in US dollars, a non-negative finite number. */
+export interface ServerToolGrant {
+  type: string;
+  perUseUsd: number;
 }
 
 /** How a relay writes its credential into the header its grant names. `'none'` writes nothing, and only with no credential (A-R21-02). */

   test('a provider given a label Docker would reject or reserve, or a value with a control character, refuses to be built (#19)', async () => {
     for (const labels of [{ 'Upper.Case': 'x' }, { 'trailing-.dot': 'x' }, { 'com.docker.compose.project': 'x' }, { 'run.owner': 'a\nb' }]) {
       expect((await refusal(() => LocalDockerProvider.create({ vaultPaths: [], labels }))).layer, JSON.stringify(labels)).toBe('labels');
     }
   });
 
   test('the stub provider, which runs commands on the host, refuses every relay', async () => {
     const stub = new StubSandboxProvider();
     expect((await refusal(() => stub.provision(specFor()))).layer).toBe('relay');
   });
 
   test('the relay itself refuses to start without a credential or a grant', async () => {
     const valid: Record<string, string> = {
       RELAY_CREDENTIAL: 'x', RELAY_UPSTREAM: UPSTREAM_ORIGIN, RELAY_PATHS: '["/v1/messages"]', RELAY_HEADER: 'x-api-key', RELAY_SCHEME: 'raw', RELAY_PORT: '8080',
-      RELAY_BUDGET: JSON.stringify(TEST_BUDGET), RELAY_METER: JSON.stringify(TEST_METER),
+      RELAY_BUDGET: JSON.stringify(TEST_BUDGET), RELAY_METER: JSON.stringify(TEST_METER), RELAY_TOOLS: '[{"type":"web_search_20250305","perUseUsd":0.01}]',
     };
     const flagsFor = (env: Record<string, string>): string[] => Object.entries(env).flatMap(([name, value]) => ['--env', `${name}=${value}`]);
 
     // The control: the same image, program, and flags with nothing missing start a relay that stays up.
     // Without it, the refusals below could be an image or a daemon that runs nothing (external review, codex-4).
     const control = `relay-start-control-${randomUUID()}`;
     upstreams.push(control);
     await run('docker', ['run', '--detach', '--name', control, ...LABEL_ARGS, ...flagsFor(valid), EGRESS_PROXY_IMAGE, 'node', '--eval', RELAY_SOURCE]);
     await new Promise((resolve) => setTimeout(resolve, 2_000));
     expect((await run('docker', ['inspect', '--format', '{{.State.Running}}', control])).stdout.trim()).toBe('true');
 
     const uncredentialed = Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(valid).filter(([name]) => name !== 'RELAY_CREDENTIAL'));
     const attempts: Array<[Record<string, string>, string]> = [
       [uncredentialed, 'no credential'],
       [{ ...valid, RELAY_PATHS: '[]' }, 'no path grant'],
       [{ ...valid, RELAY_UPSTREAM: 'http://api.example.com', RELAY_PATHS: '["/v1"]' }, 'not an https origin'],
       // P13: a relay nothing bounds, or that cannot price what it forwards, does not start either.
       [Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(valid).filter(([name]) => name !== 'RELAY_BUDGET')), 'no budget'],
       [{ ...valid, RELAY_BUDGET: JSON.stringify({ maxTokens: 0, maxCostUsd: 1 }) }, 'no budget'],
       [{ ...valid, RELAY_METER: JSON.stringify({ ...TEST_METER, dialect: 'gemini-generate' }) }, 'no meter'],
       // A-R6-02: a relay that does not know how to write its credential does not guess.
       [Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(valid).filter(([name]) => name !== 'RELAY_SCHEME')), 'no scheme'],
       [{ ...valid, RELAY_SCHEME: 'basic' }, 'no scheme'],
       [{ ...valid, RELAY_METER: JSON.stringify({ ...TEST_METER, prices: { m: { ...TEST_METER.prices[TEST_MODEL], outputPerMTok: -1 } } }) }, 'no price table'],
+      // A-R56-01: no grant, a type it cannot count, an unpriced use, a type twice, or a grant under a dialect that forwards none.
+      [Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(valid).filter(([name]) => name !== 'RELAY_TOOLS')), 'no server-tool grant'],
+      [{ ...valid, RELAY_TOOLS: '[{"type":"code_execution_20250825","perUseUsd":0.01}]' }, 'no server-tool grant'],
+      [{ ...valid, RELAY_TOOLS: '[{"type":"web_search_20250305","perUseUsd":-1}]' }, 'no server-tool grant'],
+      [{ ...valid, RELAY_TOOLS: '[{"type":"web_search_20250305","perUseUsd":0.01},{"type":"web_search_20250305","perUseUsd":0}]' }, 'no server-tool grant'],
+      [{ ...valid, RELAY_METER: JSON.stringify(TEST_OPENAI_METER) }, 'no server-tool grant'],
     ];
     for (const [env, named] of attempts) {
       const outcome = await run('docker', ['run', '--rm', ...LABEL_ARGS, ...flagsFor(env), EGRESS_PROXY_IMAGE, 'node', '--eval', RELAY_SOURCE]).then(
         () => ({ code: 0, stderr: '' }),
         (error: unknown) => ({ code: (error as { code?: unknown }).code, stderr: stderrOf(error) }),
       );
       // The relay's own refusal, naming what it lacks: not merely some non-zero exit.
       expect(outcome.code, JSON.stringify(env)).toBe(1);
       expect(outcome.stderr, JSON.stringify(env)).toContain('model-relay: ');
       expect(outcome.stderr, JSON.stringify(env)).toContain(named);
     }
   });
 });

 /** A self-signed certificate for `UPSTREAM_HOST`, valid for a day. It is its own root: the relay is given it as `relayTrust`. */
 export async function makeCertificate(): Promise<Certificate> {
   const dir = await mkdtemp(join(tmpdir(), 'relay-cert-'));
   try {
     const keyPath = join(dir, 'key.pem');
     const certPath = join(dir, 'cert.pem');
     await run(
       'openssl',
       [
         'req', '-x509', '-newkey', 'ec', '-pkeyopt', 'ec_paramgen_curve:P-256', '-nodes', '-days', '1',
         '-subj', `/CN=${UPSTREAM_HOST}`, '-addext', `subjectAltName=DNS:${UPSTREAM_HOST}`,
         '-keyout', keyPath, '-out', certPath,
       ],
       // Git for Windows' runtime rewrites an argument that starts with `/` into a Windows path.
       { env: { ...process.env, MSYS_NO_PATHCONV: '1' } },
     );
     return { cert: await readFile(certPath, 'utf8'), key: await readFile(keyPath, 'utf8') };
   } finally {
     await rm(dir, { recursive: true, force: true });
   }
 }
 
 /**
  * The upstream. It never echoes the credential: it reports whether the one it
  * was sent is the one it expected, so a test can prove the relay wrote the
  * provider's credential without the value ever travelling back into the
  * sandbox that asked.
  *
  * `/v1/messages/redirect` answers 302 to another origin, so a relay that
  * followed redirects would be caught sending the credential onward.
  *
  * `POST /v1/messages` answers as the Messages API does, with the usage a meter
  * reads (P13). What it answers is steered by `relay_test` in the request body,
  * which the relay forwards unchanged like the rest: `usage` (the four counts),
  * `omit` (`'final'` drops the output count, `'all'` every count), `status`,
- * `encoding` (`'gzip'`), `hold` (milliseconds before answering), and `hangup`
+ * `searches` (the web searches `server_tool_use` reports), `encoding` (`'gzip'`), `hold` (milliseconds before answering), and `hangup`
  * (`true` reads the whole request and closes without answering). `stream:
  * true` answers in server-sent events. It logs
  * the SHA-256 of every body it received and sent, so a suite can prove both
  * crossed the relay byte for byte.
  */
@@ -166,88 +166,90 @@ function responses(q, s, body, echo) {
   let request = {};
   try { request = JSON.parse(body.toString('utf8')); } catch (error) { request = {}; }
   const t = request.relay_test || {};
   if (t.hangup === true) { s.socket.destroy(); return; }
   const counts = t.usage || { input: 10, output: 5, cacheRead: 0, cacheWrite: 0 };
   const status = t.status || 200;
   const usage = t.omit === 'all' ? null : {
     input_tokens: counts.input + counts.cacheRead,
     input_tokens_details: { cached_tokens: counts.cacheRead },
   };
   if (usage !== null && t.omit !== 'final') usage.output_tokens = counts.output;
   const response = { id: 'resp_test', object: 'response', model: request.model, status: 'completed',
     output: [{ type: 'message', role: 'assistant', content: [{ type: 'output_text', text: JSON.stringify(echo) }] }], usage: usage };
   let payload;
   let type;
   if (status !== 200) {
     type = 'application/json';
     payload = JSON.stringify({ error: { type: 'server_error', message: 'test upstream' } });
   } else if (request.stream === true) {
     type = 'text/event-stream';
     const events = [
       ['response.created', { type: 'response.created', response: Object.assign({}, response, { status: 'in_progress', output: [], usage: null }) }],
       ['response.output_text.delta', { type: 'response.output_text.delta', delta: JSON.stringify(echo) }],
     ];
     // 'cut' ends the stream before its final event, as a dropped connection does.
     if (t.omit !== 'cut') events.push(['response.completed', { type: 'response.completed', response: response }]);
     payload = events.map(function (e) { return 'event: ' + e[0] + '\\ndata: ' + JSON.stringify(e[1]) + '\\n\\n'; }).join('');
   } else {
     type = 'application/json';
     payload = JSON.stringify(response);
   }
   const bytes = Buffer.from(payload, 'utf8');
   console.log('UPSTREAM_BODY ' + sha(body) + ' UPSTREAM_SENT ' + sha(bytes));
   s.writeHead(status, { 'content-type': type });
   s.end(bytes);
 }
 function answer(s, request, t, body, echo) {
   const counts = t.usage || { input: 10, output: 5, cacheRead: 0, cacheWrite: 0 };
   const status = t.status || 200;
   const usage = t.omit === 'all' ? undefined : {
     input_tokens: counts.input,
     cache_read_input_tokens: counts.cacheRead,
     cache_creation_input_tokens: counts.cacheWrite,
     cache_creation: { ephemeral_5m_input_tokens: counts.cacheWrite, ephemeral_1h_input_tokens: 0 },
   };
   if (usage !== undefined && t.omit !== 'final') usage.output_tokens = counts.output;
+  // A-R56-01: the uses of a server tool, reported where the final output count is.
+  if (usage !== undefined && t.omit !== 'final' && t.searches !== undefined) usage.server_tool_use = { web_search_requests: t.searches };
   let payload;
   let type;
   if (status !== 200) {
     type = 'application/json';
     payload = JSON.stringify({ type: 'error', error: { type: 'overloaded_error', message: 'test upstream' } });
   } else if (request.stream === true) {
     type = 'text/event-stream';
     const start = { id: 'msg_test', type: 'message', role: 'assistant', model: request.model, content: [] };
     if (usage !== undefined) start.usage = { input_tokens: usage.input_tokens, cache_read_input_tokens: usage.cache_read_input_tokens,
       cache_creation_input_tokens: usage.cache_creation_input_tokens, cache_creation: usage.cache_creation, output_tokens: 1 };
     const events = [
       ['message_start', { type: 'message_start', message: start }],
       ['content_block_start', { type: 'content_block_start', index: 0, content_block: { type: 'text', text: '' } }],
       ['content_block_delta', { type: 'content_block_delta', index: 0, delta: { type: 'text_delta', text: JSON.stringify(echo) } }],
       ['content_block_stop', { type: 'content_block_stop', index: 0 }],
     ];
     if (t.omit !== 'final' && t.omit !== 'all') {
-      events.push(['message_delta', { type: 'message_delta', delta: { stop_reason: 'end_turn' }, usage: { output_tokens: counts.output } }]);
+      events.push(['message_delta', { type: 'message_delta', delta: { stop_reason: 'end_turn' }, usage: { output_tokens: counts.output, server_tool_use: usage === undefined ? undefined : usage.server_tool_use } }]);
       events.push(['message_stop', { type: 'message_stop' }]);
     }
     payload = events.map(function (e) { return 'event: ' + e[0] + '\\ndata: ' + JSON.stringify(e[1]) + '\\n\\n'; }).join('');
   } else {
     type = 'application/json';
     const message = Object.assign({ id: 'msg_test', type: 'message', role: 'assistant', model: request.model }, echo);
     if (usage !== undefined) message.usage = usage;
     payload = JSON.stringify(message);
   }
   let bytes = Buffer.from(payload, 'utf8');
   const headers = { 'content-type': type };
   if (t.encoding === 'gzip') { bytes = zlib.gzipSync(bytes); headers['content-encoding'] = 'gzip'; }
   console.log('UPSTREAM_BODY ' + sha(body) + ' UPSTREAM_SENT ' + sha(bytes));
   s.writeHead(status, headers);
   // Written in pieces, as a stream arrives, so a meter reading one whole chunk is not what passes.
   const step = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(bytes.length / 4));
   let at = 0;
   (function next() {
     if (at >= bytes.length) { s.end(); return; }
     s.write(bytes.subarray(at, at + step));
     at += step;
     setTimeout(next, 5);
   })();
 }
